# HANDOFF archive

> Older material moved out of `HANDOFF.md` to keep it short. Newest first.

## Archived 2026-10-22

> Everything below, down to the next `## Archived` / `## Session log, up to 2026-10-06` heading, is `HANDOFF.md` §1–§6 exactly as it stood on `main` at `dd610787` (2026-10-22), moved verbatim when HANDOFF was cut back to ~300 lines: the old executive-summary bullets (2026-10-06 → 2026-10-22, incl. the M5-T bullets), §2 decisions, §3 debt, §4 environment, the long §5 per-breed / per-feature "Update …" paragraphs and the §6 session log (2026-10-06 → 2026-10-15). Its section headings are kept as they were.

- **2026-10-20: state videos of new pets did not play in the app** (`fix/mp4-faststart`): fal's Kling MP4s now arrive with the index (`moov`) AFTER `mdat` (older files had it first); serving was fine (200/206 over h2, verified on prod for pet 13 / look 5), but expo-video on iOS showed »This video can't be played right now« for every video, while the admin's browser played them. Fix: `Media\Mp4FastStart` (pure PHP qt-faststart — the production image has no ffmpeg; moves `moov` first, shifts `stco`/`co64`, refuses anything odd and leaves the file as is) runs in `PetMediaService::storeResult()` for every video; `media:faststart {--dry-run}` rewrites stored files in place (same path, no DB writes) and is scheduled hourly at :17 (reads only box headers of good files), so existing look/pet videos heal within an hour of the deploy. Expo-video may have cached the broken file per URL — URLs re-sign every ~30 min, so a fresh album open picks up the fixed file.
- **2026-10-19: HTTP/3 black hole** (`fix/caddy-http3-udp`): Caddy sent `Alt-Svc: h3=":443"; ma=2592000` but compose published only TCP 443 and UFW allowed only 443/tcp → iOS tried QUIC into nothing; likely cause of David's 14-min launch stall (no request reached the server). Compose now publishes `443:443/udp`; **David must run `ufw allow 443/udp`** (+ Hetzner Cloud firewall if one is attached). Verify after deploy: `curl --http3 -sI https://api.petprep.si/up` or an h3 checker.
- **2026-10-19: launch splash can no longer hang** (`fix/boot-hang-after-profile-switch`, PR #282, mobile JS only → OTA; fingerprint = main ios `0ca9398f…`, android `98911012…`): David's iPhone sat on »Loading…« ~14 min, no request reached the server. Cause: launch `GET /api/user` had no timeout (expo/fetch sets `timeoutInterval = 0` on iOS) and the `restoring` splash had no time limit / exit (`useLongOutage` never fires for a request that never settles). Fix: every restore step bounded (Keychain 3 s → `locked`, `/api/user` 8 s abort → `offline`, extras 2 s), restore + Keychain migration single-flight (a slow migration → `locked`, never a session or the login screen while the token key may be missing), `locked` on screen retries itself (0.5/1/2/4 s), 26 s watchdog (> `RESTORE_BUDGET_MS`) → offline screen, splash »Odjava« behind the parental gate, `logout()` with an unknown user keeps other children's shared sessions, late 401 after a timeout ignored. OTA ruled out. **Not verified on a phone.** Debt: why the connection stalled is unknown — check Caddy `Alt-Svc` (HTTP/3) vs compose publishing only TCP 443.
- **2026-10-19: Android payments key** (`chore/android-revenuecat-key`): public RevenueCat Android SDK key `EXPO_PUBLIC_REVENUECAT_ANDROID_KEY` in the `production` profile of `eas.json` (Android app added to the existing RevenueCat project by David; Play one-time product `petprep_challenge_12w`, purchase option `buy`, 49,99 €, EN + SL, active). `eas.json` is part of the fingerprint → ios `c57dec9f…`, android `0a3c14c4…`. Still open for David: RevenueCat Play service-account JSON, Android product in the current offering's package, Android OAuth client with the Play signing SHA-1.
- **2026-10-19: hotfix — cat routine ledger failed** (`fix/routine-type-varchar`): `pet_daily_routines.routine_type` was varchar(8) since M2-06, the cat routines `litter_scoop` (12) / `litter_change` (13) didn't fit → `RoutineLedgerService::closePet()` threw for every cat with a litter row (Postgres "value too long for type character varying(8)" at :03 / :33), the batch rolled back and the cat never closed a day (Care Score history / certificate frozen). Migration `2026_12_25_120000_widen_pet_daily_routines_routine_type` widens `routine_type` → varchar(32), `status` → varchar(16) (metadata-only in Postgres); regression test in `CatLitterScratchingGroomingTest` (fails without the migration). Backlog closes itself on the next ticks (`MAX_DAYS_PER_CLOSE` per tick). Also found: production Laravel logs live in the `app_storage` Docker volume (`docker exec backend-app-1 tail storage/logs/laravel.log`), NOT `/opt/petprep/repo/backend/storage/logs` (Sail-era leftover, last write 2026-10-01). Next: David's superadmin error-monitoring page.

- **2026-10-19: first Android EAS build fixed** (`fix/M5-F10-android-fingerprint-gs-secret`): EAS refused it ("Runtime version calculated on local machine not equal …") because the build server keeps the `GOOGLE_SERVICES_JSON` file env var outside `mobile/` under another name and the fingerprint hashed that file; `fingerprint.config.js` now ignores that path too. Verified: Android hash identical with no file / local file / EAS-style secret path (`bfafa82e…`); iOS unaffected. Next: David reruns `eas build -p android --profile production`.
- **2026-10-19: Android release lint fix** (`fix/android-locales-ios-only`): `mobile/locales/sl.json` iOS permission texts moved under `ios` — as top-level keys Expo also wrote them to Android `values-b+sl/strings.xml` and `lintVitalRelease` failed (ExtraTranslation). iOS InfoPlist output and both fingerprints unchanged; guard test `appLocales.test.ts`.
- **2026-10-17: child no longer signed out every ~2 days** (`fix/child-session-locked-keychain`, mobile JS only — OTA): David's child account landed on the login screen every ~48 h; force-quit + reopen restored it. Cause: token in SecureStore with default `WHEN_UNLOCKED`; iOS launches the app in the background while the phone is locked (step sync / pushes), `restoreSession` couldn't read the Keychain and returned `anonymous` → `reset()`. Fix: Keychain read error → `locked` (boot stays `restoring`, retried on the next foreground; 3 foreground locks in a row → login as before); session secrets (`petprep_auth_token`, child sessions) written with `AFTER_FIRST_UNLOCK`; one-time `migrateSessionKeychain` (delete + re-add — iOS never changes an existing item's access class) after a successful restore. Backend tokens never expire (`sanctum.expiration = null`) — no server change.
- **2026-10-15 (D20 disk hygiene):** server disk 30/38 GB was 23.4 GB Docker build cache (prune filter `until=168h` never matched). Deploy now caps cache at 2 GB (website 1 GB), bounded container logs 3 × 10 MB on all services, ≥ 70 % disk warning. David: one-time journald cap (PRODUCTION_DEPLOYMENT.md »Disk hygiene«); the logging change applies on the next production deploy (containers recreated).
- **2026-10-15 (perf, David: tasks too slow):** measured the Pest suite with JUnit — 82 % of its time was the per-test breed seeding (`seedLifeStageData()` before 1,641 tests: ~2,100 single-row inserts ≈ 0.9 s each). `BreedStageParamsSeeder` / `BreedConfigsSeeder::seedConfigs` now insert in multi-row `insertOrIgnore` chunks (same insert-only semantics): seeding 0.88 s → 0.12 s, full serial suite 34.6 → 13.7 min locally (3,267 tests green). - **2026-10-14: M5-F13 the app survives a backend deploy** (`feat/M5-F13-maintenance-reconnect`, mobile JS only — OTA; native fingerprint unchanged: ios `9dcce1bc…`, android `cc53883a…`): `apiRequest` reports every answer to `serverStatusStore` (Zustand, UI state) — maintenance 503 *without* `reason`/`code` / 502 / 504 start the outage, any other answer ends it; a bare network failure never starts it (phone offline ≠ deploy) but keeps an existing one up. Queries: `shouldRetry` / `shouldRetryChildPet` retry deploy errors up to 6× with `Retry-After` (clamped 1–30 s) or 2 s … 30 s; 4xx handling unchanged (401 → logout handler exactly as before, 409 / 422 / 423 unchanged). `useServerRecovery` (AppNavigator) probes `GET /up` (3–30 s / `Retry-After`, foreground only) while the banner shows and `invalidateQueries({refetchType:'active'})` once the server is back. Banner `ServerUpdatingBanner` (i18n `common:serverUpdating`, dark in the HUD / light over all parent screens, `pointerEvents="none"`, fade off with Reduce Motion); HUD without state shows `hud-updating` instead of the load-error screen; parent dashboard / child report / timeline hide their error banners during the outage; launch during a deploy → `splash-updating` and the restore re-runs by itself on recovery. Child actions: new `ActionFailure` kind `updating` (text "PetPrep se ravno posodablja…") — **mutations are never auto-retried** (feed/water/clean/take_out have no idempotency key server-side; TanStack mutations default to 0 retries, `usePlay` keeps its offline-only ≤ 2 retries within the server's 10 s dedupe). Realtime: pusher-js reconnects by itself (verified in 8.6.0 source: closed → retry 1 s, `connected` → `subscribeAll`), but a failed channel auth is never retried by pusher-js — `withOutageRetry` retries `/api/broadcasting/auth` on deploy errors (20 attempts ≈ 5–8 min, through the existing `channelAuthGate`, stops on unmount); `usePetWebSocket` / `usePetChannels` call `onResubscribed` on every re-subscription → child `['child','pet']` / parent dashboard invalidated (missed broadcasts). Tests: 2614 Jest green (+57 new), `tsc` clean. **Not verified on a phone.** New debt: (1) `/up` is Laravel's health route — fine behind Caddy, but if Caddy itself is down the probe sees a network error and waits for the next real answer (no false recovery, just slower); (2) mutations other than the care actions (parent forms) show their own existing error text on a 503, not the "updating" one; (3) no server-side idempotency key for care actions — if ever wanted, add `Idempotency-Key` and then allow a retry. Next: David tests a deploy with the app open (child HUD + parent dashboard).

Caddy `lb_try_duration 10s` holds requests during the app swap. Reverb and Caddy are not restarted when the sha256 of their inputs + `.env` + base image id matches `releases/{reverb,caddy}.fingerprint` (first D19 deploy recreates and records). Harness 333/333. **Verify on the first real deploy:** the gap while `app` restarts and whether open WebSockets survive.

- **Also 2026-10-12** (Claude — **M5-F11 water "Dolij, ko se prazni"** (`feat/M5-F11-water-refill`, David 2026-10-12): no daily water cap any more — water is allowed when the live thirst shows ≤ new `breed_configs.water_refill_below` (60 for every breed, CHECK 1–100, Filament field EN + SL) AND the existing gap passed (dog 180 / cat 240 min); refusal stays `water_too_soon` with `next_allowed_at` = later of gap end and the forecast threshold minute (`App\Services\DecayMath`, the tick's formula — `PetDecayService` now uses it too), so installed app builds show the time without an update; `water_daily_limit` is never emitted (enum kept); `water_times_per_day` = score minimum only; child state `water.refill_below` added, `remaining_today` = expected refills open for the score. Dog regression snapshot re-recorded on purpose (water answers changed). **Debt:** the mobile still has the `water_daily_limit` text and shows "jutri" when `next_allowed_at` is missing (frozen pet) — harmless; a mobile follow-up could show "pri ≤ 60 %". ## 2. Decisions (2026-10-02)

- **Business model:** 12-week PetPrep Challenge **49.99 €** ~~with a 7-day free trial~~ — **no free trial since 2026-10-08 10:28 (David, M3-13): the free mutt is the try-out, the challenge starts with a purchase**; **Mutt stays free forever**. Proposed free/paid split in `docs/business/BUSINESS_MODEL.md` §7 (awaiting David's confirmation of the split).
- **Child login:** PIN only, no child email (M2-02) — backend + app built 2026-10-04 (`feat/M2-02-pin-only-child`).
- **Monorepo:** `pet-prep-mobile` merged into `pet-prep/mobile` — done.
- **Languages:** English (default) + Slovenian, more later (M1-18).
- **Package manager:** yarn 1 (root `package.json` declares it) — cleanup in M0-05.
- **Thresholds follow the displayed value** (David, 2026-10-03): warning/escalation thresholds, pet state and zero tracking (`*_zero_since` → phase 3, illness, game over) compare the rounded half-up value the child sees (`Pet::displayMetric`): 30.4 shows 30 → phase 1; 0.4 shows 0 → counts as zero. PRODUCT_SPEC §6.
- **Interim hygiene 1.5 %/h kept until M1-05** (David, 2026-10-03) — removed on `feat/M1-04-energy-hygiene-breeds` (random hygiene events replace it).

## 3. Known bugs & debt (top items — full list in AUDIT, DEPLOYMENT.md and the archive)

1. **TestFlight 1.24.4 still loops `POST /api/devices`** (iOS echoes the push token) — fixed on `main` (mobile) + own `throttle:devices` on the server, so the game is no longer blocked; the installed build still drains battery until a new build ships.
2. **Push registration edge case:** on Android, a real token rotation arriving as the first event inside the 10 s window is taken as the echo → registered only at next app start / login.
3. **No crash reporter:** render errors only reach the device console (`logRenderError`); Sentry needs David's OK (M5-05).
4. **Training:** schedule is sent to the app, so a modified app can fake praise taps — **accepted by David** (only system security matters); taps before an app restart are lost on resume; potty "asked to go out" only logged, no timeline row; minors m3/m4 (catch-up accidents use today's potty progress; decay applied after later gains) open in DECISIONS.
5. **Tests / tooling:** Pint fails repo-wide on 9 untouched files; two Pest runs in one checkout share `storage/framework/testing/disks` (use separate worktrees); cold-cache Jest timeout in `ChildHudScreen.behaviour.test.tsx`; GitHub Actions: `actions/checkout@v4` on deprecated Node 20, `ubuntu-latest` moves to Ubuntu 26 on 2026-10-19 — check CI after that date.
6. **i18n (M1-18) not verified on a device:** Hermes plural rules (polyfill `@formatjs/intl-pluralrules` loads only if needed), Android channel names after a switch, iOS Slovenian permission texts — all need the new native build. English copy (contract, push, legal/deletion texts, terms like "pup", "Dog school", "Mixed breed", "Place") awaits David's read-through. Terms / privacy URLs are the same for both languages.
7. **Security / hygiene still open:** test accounts in production DB (M0-15), EAS signing passwords in old repo history (M0-12), RevenueCat payments built but not live (D7a checklist: store products, webhook secret, SDK keys, then `PAYMENTS_ENFORCED=true`), PHP 8.3 prod vs 8.5 Sail (M0-11).
8. **About 40 decisions marked "čaka Davida"** in DECISIONS.md (incl. 7 behaviour questions from M5-R02: chewing 0.5, accidents vs. score, midnight clock, hard stop, quiet hours, walk for all breeds, adopted dogs).

## 4. Environment & configuration

- Local: Laravel Sail; `backend/.env` and `mobile/.env` exist (gitignored). `mobile/credentials.json` local only (gitignored).
- `FAL_AI_API_KEY` set locally (not on server yet). There is **no** fal webhook secret any more (ED25519 signature). Pending: RevenueCat, push, Sentry.
- Production queue worker must run for reference images (`queue` container exists).
- Production docs: `docs/PRODUCTION_DEPLOYMENT.md`, `docs/PRODUCTION_ENV.md`; findings: `docs/engineering/DEPLOYMENT.md`.
- SSH key of David's second Mac added to the server.
- **Cloud sessions (no Docker / Sail):** PostgreSQL 16 runs locally on `127.0.0.1:5432` (postgres / postgres). Run Pest with `DB_CONNECTION=pgsql DB_HOST=127.0.0.1 DB_DATABASE=testing_<branch> DB_USERNAME=postgres DB_PASSWORD=postgres php vendor/bin/pest --parallel`; in a worktree copy `vendor/` with `rsync -a --exclude=.git backend/vendor/ <worktree>/backend/vendor/` and delete it afterwards (disk is limited). The main checkout's `mobile/node_modules` is outdated (no `expo-notifications`) → `yarn install --frozen-lockfile` in the worktree.
- **Composer as root (cloud):** run `COMPOSER_ALLOW_SUPERUSER=1 composer install`, otherwise plugins are skipped, `vendor/pest-plugins.json` is missing and `pest --parallel` fails with "Unknown option". Start Postgres with `service postgresql start` and set the postgres password (`ALTER USER postgres PASSWORD 'postgres'`).
- **GitHub:** private repo on the free plan → no branch protection; the single gate is the `CI OK` check run. Actions logs can't be downloaded through the cloud proxy — read check-run **annotations** (the `plan` job prints its decision as a `::notice title=plan::`).

**Update 2026-10-12 (M5-R09b »Ali si vedel?« breed facts — server + admin, branch `feat/M5-R09b-breed-facts-backend`, PR open, not merged; spec `docs/product/DID_YOU_KNOW_SPEC.md`):** migration `2026_12_04_120000_create_breed_facts` (additive, reversible): `breed_facts` (key unique, breed = BreedType value without enum CHECK but `NOT IN (mutt, domestic_cat)`, category / audience / status CHECKs, family + health ⇒ parent, approved ⇔ approved_at, content_hash), `breed_fact_reads` (unique user + fact), `breed_fact_shows` (unique child + pet + local date, program_week). `BreedFactService` (who sees what, reads, fact of the day, `autoShowRefusal` order no_pet › non_pedigree › locked › no_image › no_fact › first_day › quiet_hours › shown_today › week_cap › too_soon), `BreedFactPayload` (Scramble types), `BreedFactController`, `BreedFactPolicy`, `BreedFactsRequest`; routes `GET /api/breeds/{breed}/facts` + `POST /api/breed-facts/{fact}/read` (`ability:parent,child`), `GET /api/child/fact-of-the-day` + `POST /api/child/fact-of-the-day/shown` (child group). No ClientFeature (additive endpoints). **Content:** canonical `docs/research/breed-facts/<breed>.json` (format: its README), shipped byte-for-byte copy `backend/database/data/breed-facts/` (the production image is `backend/` only) — `BreedFactImportTest` fails when they differ, `scripts/ci-plan.sh` runs backend tests for `docs/research/breed-facts/**`; `php artisan breeds:import-facts {--breed=*} {--dry-run} {--print-hashes} {--dir=}` (manual; a file with any error or unknown key is rejected whole; `approved` in a file refused; `verified` only with a matching `verified_hash`, else draft; unchanged content keeps its status / approval; changed content → verified-with-proof or draft, approval cleared). **Admin:** `/admin/breed-facts` (EN + SL) — filters, verify / approve (superadmin) / back to draft, bulk approve of verified rows, view page with child + parent card preview. Export: `breed_facts.{reads, auto_shows}`; game-data reset keeps `breed_facts`, wipes reads / shows. `mobile/src/api/schema.ts` regenerated (additive; spec exported with `scramble:export` against a local Postgres and served to `scripts/generate-api-types.mjs`). **Next:** R09a content agent writes `docs/research/breed-facts/border_collie.json` / `maine_coon.json` → copy to backend → deploy → `breeds:import-facts --dry-run`, then import → David approves in the admin; then R09c (child sheet, chip, Moja pasma — calm moment in the app) and R09d (parent card + guide). **Update 2026-10-12 (post-merge review of PRs #195 / #197 / #199 / #200, branch `fix/M5-R16-R17-review-copy`):** independent `qa-reviewer` approved all four, no blockers. Fixed here: the digestion note no longer says "about an hour" / "a big dog" (the gap is David's editable game value, not care advice — the "until HH:MM" line already says when); a parent build that does not know an activity type shows "dejavnost" / "activity" instead of the raw code; SL socialisation hint wording (»… ali se pelje z avtom«). **Known debt:** a second child on an old app build who joins an existing grooming-enabled dog gets fair-share grooming routines without a »Počeši« button (same gap as training). ~~the socialisation report can show weeks before a child joined / during a hard stop / after game over as "missed"~~ → **resolved 2026-10-12** (David: excused weeks; per-child rows from the caretaker start).

**Update 2026-10-12 (M5-R17 puppy socialisation, branch `feat/M5-R17-puppy-socialisation`, PR #200; David 2026-10-12 "tedenska naloga"):** `SocialisationService` + `SocialisationPayload` (child state + `PetUpdated` `socialisation`), `POST /api/child/pet/socialisation {experience: person|dog|sound|place}` (`SocialiseRequest`, `PetActivityService::socialise`, activity `socialised_pet`, value = experience code), child report `socialisation` (weeks done / open / missed, `scored: false`), gate `pets.socialisation_enabled` (migration `2026_11_30_120000`, ClientFeature `socialisation`, set at creation when both apps declared it — **existing dogs have no task**; David must create a new puppy on the new build to see it). New stage key `socialisation_tasks_per_week` = 1 on the **puppy** stage for 12 breeds (`BreedStageParamsSeeder::DOG_SOCIALISATION`, verbatim PDSA quotes in `data.json proposed_game_parameters.<breed>.socialisation`): Labrador S53, German Shepherd S100, Beagle S115, Standard Poodle S122, Australian Shepherd S134, Havanese S140, Husky S162, Rottweiler S169, Bulldog S180, Cane Corso S199, Pembroke Welsh Corgi S206, Dobermann S211. A week has a task when its starting age (arrival + week index) is a puppy age — a 2-month arrival gets 7 weeks. One per week per pet, not in quiet hours. **Deliberately not a `RoutineLedgerService` routine** (every ledger routine is scored); `PuppySocialisationTest` proves the report's Care Score / light / by_type are identical with the week done or missed. App: "Nova izkušnja" chip + `SocialisationSheet` (four options, one tap, optimistic), parent report row "Nova izkušnja (mladiček)" + "ne vpliva na oceno skrbi", timeline "Nova izkušnja: nov zvok · Mia", i18n namespace `socialisation` (EN + SL); schema.ts regenerated (additive). Runbook §3 "Puppy socialisation" row. The migration widens both CHECK constraints from their current definitions (safe against the parallel M5-R16 migration). **Resolved by David 2026-10-12** (see the routine-decisions update above): (1) all puppies get the task; (2) moot — every dog breed has the row; (3) blocked weeks are "excused"; (4) existing dogs stay without (no backfill). Not verified on a device.

**Update 2026-10-12 (prey-drive chip + Dobermann answers, branch `feat/M5-R10-prey-drive-chip`, PR #197; David 2026-10-12):** new `consider` key `chases_small_pets` (EN "strong prey drive — may chase small pets", SL »močan lovski nagon — lahko lovi manjše živali«) in `config/breed_suitability.php` + `BreedCatalogResource` union + `picker.ts` `CONSIDER_TAGS` / `FALLBACK_CATALOGUE` + `pet.json` (en, sl); appended last to French Bulldog (S81), Dachshund (S127), Westie (S147), Siberian Husky (S162), Dobermann (S211) — refs `<breed>.behaviour.other_pets` (+ `dobermann.suitability.pdsa_other_pets`); not Beagle / Bernese / Rottweiler / Cavalier / German Shepherd (supervision advice only). `BreedSuitabilityTest` asserts exactly these five; schema.ts regenerated (additive); registry export regenerated. Runbook §3: future breeds get the chip when a breed quote names a high prey drive. Dobermann answers recorded (DECISIONS, data.json): senior **83 stays** until McMillan 2024 is reachable (no seeder change); SL »doberman« confirmed; FCI colours only confirmed; no extra marketing limits. **Website:** `pet-prep-website` needs the `chases_small_pets` label words + a registry re-sync (own PR). Not verified on a device.

**Update 2026-10-12 (M5-R10-25 Dobermann / doberman, branch `feat/M5-R10-25-dobermann`, PR #191):** twenty-first paid dog, the seventh breed appended after the 20 (AKC 2024 No. 16 "Doberman Pinscher", S174; queue row 25). Dog touch points as the Pembroke Welsh Corgi: migration `2026_11_27_120000` widens only `pets_breed_type_check`; `DOG_BREEDS` + `dobermannProfile()` (`CONFIRMED_R10_DOBERMANN`): stages 9 / 36 / **83** (0.75 × VetCompass median 9.2 y, O'Neill et al. 2013, S212 — only 37 deaths; **provisional** until McMillan 2024 is checked — the McMillan / Dogs Trust tables were not reachable; alternatives 90 RKC "Over 10 years" S209, 84 Dogs Trust > 7 y S14), arrival 2 / 9 / 36 / 83, adult 120 min (RKC S209 "More than 2 hours per day"; PDSA S211 "a minimum of two hours") → 12,000 steps, puppy 10 min × age capped at 120 (12 months), senior 90 → 9,000, learning × 1.9 (Coren 5, Brightest, S35), FCI 143 (S207, 2015; nomenclature S208) Group 2 Section 1, Germany, weight 32–45 kg (males about 40–45, females about 32–35), height 68–72 / 63–68 cm, growth end 15–18 months (S10 Large), RKC size Large, sort 210; Border Collie care rates. Suitability: suits active_family, family_pet (RKC "an excellent family dog"), large_home (RKC "Large house" / "Large garden"); consider long_daily_exercise, not_for_small_children (PDSA S211 "we wouldn’t recommend a Dobermann if you have young children"), experienced_owner (PDSA: probably not for first-time owners), needs_mental_stimulation, sheds — no new vocabulary key; no welfare chip (RKC Breed Watch Category 1) → in marketing. Register: health keys hip_dysplasia_arthritis, **new** `wobbler_syndrome`, heart_muscle_disease (dilated cardiomyopathy), bleeding_disorder_vwd, **new** `eye_phpv`, bloat_gdv, **new** `certain_cancers`, **new** `hypothyroidism` — no numbers; lifespan = RKC band "over 10 years" only (the 37-death median is not exported as a fact (only inside the senior-boundary decision string, like every breed)). The new health keys and `dna_dings2` need EN + SL words on the website (website PR follows). **Open for David:** (2) M5-R16 meal–exercise gap and M5-R17 socialisation now also list the Dobermann (M5-R13 dog brushing is built); (7) the RKC breed page used is the older address (display.aspx?id=5121, S209) — the current page was not reachable. **Resolved by David 2026-10-12:** (1) senior 83 stays until McMillan 2024 is reachable; (3) SL name »doberman« confirmed; (4) no extra marketing limits needed; (5) prey drive → new chip `chases_small_pets`; (6) FCI colours only confirmed.

Appearance from WCF BEN 2024-08-01 (C42, quotes) + FIFe BEN 01.01.2026 (C43, tool summary only) + FIFe colours via World Cat Congress (C47): brown / silver / snow, spotted (rosettes) or marbled, spotted belly; never white patches, vertical stripes or ear tufts; blue eyes only with snow; feature "a domestic cat, not a wild cat"; portrait default brown rosetted. Register health keys `pra_pk_def` (new website words), `heart_hcm`, `kneecap_luxation`, `cancer_risk`; breeder checklist `listed` — `dna_pra_b` (new website word) + `dna_pk_def` minimum (GCCF Active Register, K22). **Open for David:** (1) **marketing** — the Bengal descends from the Asian leopard cat; FelineVMA (C48) opposes wild × domestic breeding and discourages F1–F3 ownership, The Cat Group incl. BSAVA (C50) does not support breeding / keeping wild cat hybrids; neither advises against advertising registered domestic Bengals, so the runbook rule keeps it in marketing ("only as a registered domestic breed, never wild, never easy for small children") — decide whether to use it at all; (2) PetMD: "may not be the best fit for families with small children" is register text only (cats have no suitability tags yet); (3) colour weights unsourced; (4) PDSA / PetMD say the Bengal needs plenty of stimulation and exercise — built as the general cat play / scratching rules (no sourced number for more play); a higher play goal for very active cat breeds would be a new rule; (5) website words for the new keys `pra_pk_def` and `dna_pra_b` come with the website PR (the registry build fails without them); (6) the C14 value 8.51 y is the 2026-10-08 capture (BBC Science Focus confirms 8.5 y; PDSA says 12–16 y — a different measure).

**Update 2026-10-11 (cat grooming for every sourced breed, branch `feat/M5-R10-cat-grooming`, PR #169):** David's standing rule — every sourced breed need is built in the app, never left as "proposal, not built" (DECISIONS, runbook §3 + §6 item 10). British Shorthair grooming 1× per week (VCA C28), Ragdoll 2× per week (upper bound of PetMD C32), matted coat when at least half of the week's slots are missed (`CatChoreService::mattedAfterMissed()` replaces `MATTED_AFTER_MISSED`; Maine Coon still 2 of 3). Register: `grooming_rule` for both breeds (the website shows it on the next export). The branch name holds the M5-R10 lock while open.

A new country = items with its `country` + a locale whose `checklistCountry.code` matches. Genetics added as planned on home / AI assistant / investors (no own-test, lab or price details).

**Update 2026-10-10 (M5-R10-13 Siberian Husky / sibirski haski, branch `feat/M5-R10-13-siberian-husky`, PR #161; portrait PR #162, website PR #27):** fourteenth paid dog breed, tenth unattended run on `docs/engineering/ADD_BREED_RUNBOOK.md` §3 — every game value is a standing rule (`proposed_game_parameters.siberian_husky.*.decision`; seeder constant `CONFIRMED_R10_SIBERIAN_HUSKY`). Edge cases: lifespan — McMillan 2024 names no Siberian Husky in its text (S135 / S54), its Supplementary Table 3 and the Dogs Trust breed table (S123) were not reachable, Teng 2022's Husky life table was not reachable, no VetCompass median → the RKC lower bound "Over 10 years" (S160, PDSA S162 the same) → senior 90 months, **provisional** (alternatives: 88 from the Finnish average 9.75 y, IPFD S165 — not a UK median; 84 from Dogs Trust > 7 y); **new consider key `secure_fencing`** (runbook new-tag rule: PDSA S162 "Owners need a very secure garden with high fences …"); no family_pet / children / small_children (PDSA: adult-only or teenage households, not with smaller children), no other_pets (prey drive), no often_alone (separation anxiety); size label RKC "Medium-Large" (PDSA says "Large") while growth end uses the PetMD Medium class (12–15 months, FCI 15.5–28 kg); RKC Breed Watch "Category 2" lists only "Too fat" / "Too thin" (show body condition) → not a conformation welfare breed, may be used in marketing (never shown as an easy dog for small children); register export got FCI group 5 (Spitz and primitive types, section 1 Nordic Sledge Dogs) and a new size value `medium_large` (website words needed), origin US (exists), health keys `hip_dysplasia`, `inherited_eye_disease`, `epilepsy` (all exist). Portrait + register (2026-10-11): the first portrait dispatch (run 38090284398) was refused — AI Lab daily budget ($3.00 of $3.00, UTC day) used up; the run kept the queue lock with draft PR #162 and retried after the UTC reset: run 38097387532 (grey and white with a white mask, blue eyes, verdict OK — realistic photo, one adult dog, plain fog-grey backdrop, erect ears, brush tail, natural proportions) — only the Husky webp + manifest entry taken (the run regenerated the Labrador again; main's Labrador kept), portrait PR #162, website PR #27 (new FCI group 5 words, size `medium_large` words + `SIZE_ORDER` in CatalogueExplorer / views). **Open for David:** verify the McMillan 2024 Siberian Husky median (senior 90 is provisional; replace via Filament — the seeder is insert-only); the PDSA quotes (S162) were capped at ~120 characters by the fetch tool — spot-check them; confirm the `secure_fencing` wording.

### 2026-10-09 (cloud, backend-engineer) — M4-10 shared appearance pool for free pets (branch `feat/M4-10-free-pet-look-pool`, PR #108)
- **Why:** every free pet cost 1.27 $ per life stage (0.15 $ image + 2 × 0.56 $ video) with no revenue. David 2026-10-09: free pets take a look from a pool of 20 per free breed; media generated once per look and reused; a family never gets the same look twice while one is free; a purchase keeps the look; paid breeds unchanged.
- **Built:** migration `2026_10_29_120000` — `pet_looks` (breed, `pool_index` unique per breed = fill guard, `trait_fingerprint` unique per breed, `dna` jsonb), `pets.pet_look_id`, `pet_media.pet_look_id` / `look_media_id`, `pet_media.pet_id` nullable (CHECK: exactly one owner; look rows need `life_stage`), `pet_media_slot_unique` now partial (`WHERE pet_id IS NOT NULL`) + new `pet_media_look_slot_unique (pet_look_id, kind, state, life_stage)`, `pet_media_history` unique `(pet_id, storage_path)` instead of global. `Media\PetLookPoolService` (appliesTo / assignTo / counts; lazy fill with insert-or-ignore on the next index; family rule via trait fingerprints) called from `PairingService::createPet` for profiled free-breed pets with DNA v2. `PetMediaService` "Shared looks": `lookSlot()`, `referenceImageFromLook()`, `generateLookImage()` (edit of the look's latest earlier-stage image or text-to-image with the look seed, no origin cue), `videoFromLook()` / `submitLookVideo()` (`FalAiService::submitStateVideoFor`), `awaitLook()` / `linkToLook()` (copy path, one broadcast, `queueStateVideos` after an image), `fanOutReady()` from `storeResult()` of a look row (files at `looks/{id}/{kind}-{stage}-g{n}`; never replaced / deleted), `fanOutFailure()` from `fail()`; sweep skips look images (waiting pets drive them); `regenerate()` refuses pool pets; `planMissing()` costs only missing look media. `PetAppearancePrompt::stageEditPromptFor()` (dog output byte-identical — snapshot test green). `PetMediaController::SAFE_RELATIVE_PATH` accepts `looks/N/…` (Caddy X-Accel). `ReferenceImageRetryService::canRetry` + `growFrom` history checks scoped per pet. `GameDataResetService` deletes `pet_looks`. AI Lab line "Free-pet look pool (M4-10)" (looks per breed + fill cost via `MediaLabService::lookPoolFillCostUsd`). Config `media.look_pool.enabled|size` (`AI_LOOK_POOL_ENABLED`, `AI_LOOK_POOL_SIZE` 20).
- **Tests:** new `FreePetLookPoolTest` (17): lazy fill to 20 then reuse, family uniqueness (pool full / filling), paid breed + legacy profile + pool off unchanged, domestic cat pool (no "dog"), reuse with no HTTP at all (`Http::preventStrayRequests`, request count unchanged, ledger unchanged, no files under the pet dir), purchase keeps look + 4 extra videos stored on the look + next purchase 0 calls, stage transition (edit once, reuse, history + growth album), concurrent image + video guard (1 fal call), budget failure fan-out + single retry call, GDPR family delete + pet delete keep look files / export has links, Caddy path, admin regenerate refused, plan cost 0, fill cost line, prompt parity. `ResetGameDataTest` seeds a look (+1 file). ### 2026-10-08 evening (cloud, orchestrator) — deploy of #91 failed: backup retention vs. pre-reset/
- PR #91 (M5-R06-01) merged; the deploy failed twice. Merging it deploys #91 + #92.

### 2026-10-08 morning (cloud, backend-engineer) — M5-F08 quiet hours default
- **Bug (David, production iPhone):** `walk_reminder` at 00:00 local + hygiene `illness_triggered` ~03:30 although the app showed quiet hours. **Root cause (confirmed by Pest on the old code):** the family had no `quiet_hours` row; `QuietHoursCard` silently showed form defaults (21–07, on) when GET returned `null`.
- **Fix (PR #80, `fix/quiet-hours-default`, not merged):** `QuietHours::DEFAULTS` (bedtime 21:00–07:00, no school, active) created by `FamilyService::addMember()` for the first parent of a family; data migration `2026_10_22_120000` inserts it for families without a row (insert-only, first parent without a row, idempotent, `down()` no-op); `Pet::quietHours()` never null (code fallback for parentless families, same in `RoutineLedgerService`). Walk reminder floor `PushTiming::walkReminderFloor()` (stored night end + 2 h, else 09:00) also with quiet hours off. GET quiet-hours + dashboard return what applies (`saved` flag; defaults with `id: null`, `saved: false` without a row; schema.ts regenerated). App: "Default times apply — tap Save to confirm or change them" for `saved: false` / `null` (EN + SL); `DEFAULT_TIMES` no longer suggests a school window. Night window for the walk floor also = bedtime starting 00:00–04:00 (QA m1).
- **Tests:** the change first broke 212 tests (187 = `QuietHours::create()` on a parent that now already has a row, 25 = implicit "no quiet hours") → new helpers `setQuietHours()` (replaces `QuietHours::create()` in tests) and `withoutQuietHours()` in `tests/Pest.php`; wall-clock tests checked with a temporary forced `setTestNow` at 01:30 / 21:30 / 07:30 local (no new time-of-day flakiness).
- **Confirmed by David 2026-10-08 08:54:** the default (21:00–07:00, no school, active) and the walk-reminder floor (night end + 2 h, else 09:00, also with quiet hours off).
- **Debt:** parentless families can't store a row (`quiet_hours.parent_id` NOT NULL) — code fallback only; on deploy day today's live routines / running hygiene & potty clocks use the new night (closed days untouched); `pint --test` on the whole backend still fails on 7 untouched legacy files.

### 2026-10-06 (cloud, devops) — CI deploy skipped after PR #50 (`ci/plan-diagnostics`)
- **Symptom:** run 37467604915 (main 52224bc): plan `deploy=true`, suites skipped via markers, `CI OK` green, **Deploy skipped**. **Root cause:** `deploy`'s `if:` had no status function → implicit `success()`, which GitHub evaluates over all upstream jobs transitively, so the planned-skipped suites behind `CI OK` skipped it. The script was right (re-running `ci-plan.sh` against the real API on 52224bc gives `deploy=true`). Nothing server-relevant was undeployed.
- **Fix:** `if: !cancelled() && needs.plan.result == 'success' && needs.ci-ok.result == 'success' && needs.plan.outputs.deploy == 'true' && …`; plan base = newest green `main` run whose "Deploy to Hetzner Production" job succeeded (so the next backend-relevant push after this merge re-diffs from 66fcd40 and deploys); fail closed on a failing / empty-but-different diff; decision printed as `::notice title=plan::` annotation. Harness 183/183 (incident replay A deployed → B green with skipped deploy → C docs: base A, deploy), actionlint + shellcheck clean. **Not verified on GitHub** — after merge, the `main` run's plan annotation should show `base=66fcd40…` (or this merge's predecessor that deployed) and `deploy=true`, and Deploy must run.

### 2026-10-06 (cloud, devops) — faster CI pipeline (`ci/faster-pipeline`)
Branch from `origin/main` @ e8a1e14. David: "ne čakamo 2× na isti pipeline". CI only; no server change.
- **What changed:** new job `plan` (`scripts/ci-plan.sh`) → suites run only for changed paths; on a push to `main` a suite is skipped when a successful same-repo PR run of this workflow ran it on the **identical git tree** (marker artifacts `ci-green-<suite>-<tree>`, uploaded by each suite on `pull_request`); new always-reported job **`CI OK`** is the single required status; `deploy` needs `plan` + `CI OK` (no `always()`) and runs only when `backend/`, `deployment/`, `scripts/` or the workflow changed since the last green `main` run (or "Run workflow" / "Re-run all jobs"). Pest runs `--parallel`; Composer download cache; job timeouts. Full design + safety argument: DEPLOYMENT.md D16; PRODUCTION_DEPLOYMENT.md §4 + §7 (rollback = **Re-run all jobs**).
- **Verified:** `actionlint` 1.7.12 clean (incl. shellcheck of `run:` blocks); shellcheck clean on all four scripts; `scripts/tests/ci-plan.test.sh` 112/112 (PR path filter cases, main diff vs last green run, cancelled/failed run in between, API errors, non-ancestor base, re-run, markers: success / failure / cancelled / in progress / push run / other workflow / fork / other tree); `deploy-production.test.sh` 189/189 unchanged. Pest on local PostgreSQL 16 (1040 tests): serial 288 s; `--parallel` 186 s (2 processes) and 189 s (4 processes, `--recreate-databases`) — green every time, per-process DBs `<db>_test_N` created as expected.
- **Not verified:** the workflow on GitHub itself (needs a PR + a merge): the first PR run uploads markers, the first `main` push after it should show "already green on the PR" in the plan summary. If anything looks off, "Run workflow" on `main` = old behaviour (test everything + deploy).
- **Gate:** free private plan → no branch protection; the merge helper must gate on the **`CI OK`** check run (not on per-job checks, which are skipped by design).
- **QA fixes (PR #50, changes requested):** (1) `docs/research/dog-data/**` now runs backend Pest (LifeStageDataTest reads it) — separate flag: no image build, no deploy; (2) deploy guard `scripts/ci-deploy-guard.sh` as the first deploy step — push runs and every re-run **fail** unless the commit is still `main`'s head on origin (dispatch attempt 1 not checked); rollbacks = revert commit or "Run workflow" (PRODUCTION_DEPLOYMENT §7 updated); (3) expired marker artifacts ignored; (4) only `backend/README.md` / `backend/CLAUDE.md` are ignored (other `.md` under backend count); (5) D16: no branch protection on the free private plan → merge helper gates on the `CI OK` check run. Harness now 150/150 (dog-data, md, expired marker, 8 guard cases against a bare origin); actionlint + shellcheck clean.
- **Debt / risks:** markers trusted 14 days; time/random-dependent tests could pass on the PR and fail on `main` (would have been flaky anyway); a push run overtaken by a newer merge ends red at the deploy guard (benign).

### 2026-10-06 (cloud, mobile-engineer) — push-registration follow-ups (`fix/push-registration-followups`)
Branch from `origin/main` @ 5571ad6. Follow-ups from the QA review of hotfix PR #45. Mobile only.
- **Rotation not lost (`modules/push/pushRegistration.ts#handlePushTokenEvent`):** the first token event inside our own fetch window (run in flight or its 10 s grace) is the echo and becomes the known device token; a *different* token is a real rotation — while a run is in flight it sets `rotationPending` and the run registers once more after it settles; in the grace window after a run it registers right away. Still one in-flight run, (session, Expo token) dedupe and the `devicesGate` limiter; the follow-up's own echo carries the known token → no further run.
- **Stale run after logout:** `resetPushRegistration()` bumps `state.generation`; a run / retry timer from an older generation no longer marks the old session registered, stores its token in SecureStore, counts a failure, schedules a retry or starts the rotation follow-up (returns `{status:'failed', reason:'server'}`).
- **Error boundaries (`navigation/AppNavigator.tsx`):** new `utils/logRenderError.ts` (console only; logs scope + error name/message + component stack, never props / store — no PII, no SDK) used by the root `ErrorBoundary` and both `HudErrorBoundary`s (`child-hud`, `locked-overlay`). `LockedScreen` is now wrapped in `HudErrorBoundary overlay` (absolute full-screen layer, `pointerEvents="box-none"`): a crash there shows the retry card over the HUD instead of the app-wide "Nekaj je šlo narobe"; the session and the HUD stay mounted.
- **Tests:** +8 (958 total, 85 suites) — rotation during an in-flight POST registers once after it (`getExpoPushTokenAsync` ×2, no third run), rotation inside the grace window, stale 429 after logout schedules no retry and doesn't block the next session, stale success doesn't store the token; HUD error logged via the render-error path without the child's name, LockedScreen crash caught + logged + retry, `logRenderError` unit. `yarn test` + `npx tsc --noEmit` green.
- **Known limitation (vet end):** the child may stay on "pri veterinarju" up to **~5 min after `until`** when the server tick that ends the illness is late — the boundary refetch backs off 30 s → 60 s → … → 5 min while the server state doesn't change (`refetchGovernor`, hotfix PR #45). Realtime `pet.updated` ends it immediately when the socket is live.
- **Needs a device:** iOS dev / TestFlight build — sign in with notification permission, confirm one `POST /api/devices` in the Caddy log; trigger a lock state and check the overlay renders exactly as before (boundary layer must not change layout / touches).
- **New debt:** (1) a real rotation arriving as the *first* token event inside the 10 s grace window with no echo before it (Android doesn't echo) is taken as the echo and not registered until the next app start / login; (2) the render-error log goes to the device console only — no crash reporter until David approves one; (3) first test in `ChildHudScreen.behaviour.test.tsx` can exceed the 20 s timeout on a cold Jest cache on a 2-core machine (12.5 s cold here) — passes warm.
### 2026-10-06 (cloud, backend-engineer) — flaky Pest fix (`fix/flaky-pest`)
- **Flaky test:** `FamilyModelTest › births an unborn shared pet at the first contract only` (CI "Backend tests" failed once on 84a5324, passed on rerun; CI logs not reachable from the cloud sandbox, reproduced locally: 1 in 60 runs with a random `APP_KEY`, deterministic with the failing key → feed answered 422 `needs_cleaning`, hygiene 0). **Why:** birth (`Pet::giveBirth`) resets `hygiene_scheduled_through`, so the paired pet got random poops; the poop RNG is salted with `APP_KEY`, which CI generates fresh per run → in ~2 % of runs a poop fell into the 10 min between birth and the feed. **Fix:** the test disables hygiene events after the birth; `Tests\TestCase` pins the hygiene / chewing RNG salt for the whole suite (`RNG_SALT`, contextual binding — `useHygieneSalt()` / `useBehaviourSalt()` still override), so CI runs are reproducible whatever `APP_KEY` is. Found by instrumenting the RNG: the only other tests drawing from the unpinned RNG are insensitive to it, except `BehaviourEventTest › broadcasts once per take-out` (~0.15 %: teething chew in 04:00–04:02) → chewing off there.
- **Second flake (found while verifying):** `LifeStageDataTest › lists the data in Filament…` — `BreedStageParamResource` sorted only by `breed_slug`, so the 6 mutt `sleep_hours` rows came back in any order and the planted senior row was sometimes on page 2 (10 per page). Fix: `->defaultKeySort()` (stable tie-break on id — also fixes repeating/skipped rows across admin pages; same for `ActivityLogResource`), test shows 50 rows per page.
- **New test:** `devices` limiter is 10/min keyed `devices:{user id}` (IP for guests) outside testing (`PushNotificationTest`, env swapped to production in the test); `tests/Unit/TestSuiteRngSaltTest.php` (salt pinned whatever `APP_KEY`).
- **Runs (local PostgreSQL 16, PHP 8.3):** before the fix 4 full runs green (default, random seeds 1111 / 2222, instrumented) and 2 `faketime` runs (CI start 2026-10-06 10:25:38 UTC, 21:58 UTC) with no time-related failure (only `Storage::fake` collisions from two suites running in parallel in one checkout); after all fixes full suite green in default order and with `--order-by=random --random-order-seed=4444` (1040 tests); the fixed test green with 40 random `APP_KEY`s even with the salt pin disabled.
- **Debt:** two Pest processes in one checkout share `storage/framework/testing/disks/*` (`Storage::fake`) — run parallel suites in separate worktrees.

### 2026-10-06 (cloud, mobile-engineer) — hotfix: push-registration loop, child refetch limits, 429, HUD error boundary (`fix/mobile-child-refetch-loop`)
Incident (TestFlight 1.24.4 from main incl. PR #39/#40/#43): legacy mutt (pet 3) at the vet until 10:41:01 Ljubljana; Caddy logged **429** on `GET /api/child/pet` at 08:40:16, 08:41:15, 08:41:24, 08:42:01 UTC; iOS "PetPrep crashed"; after relaunch the dead "Kužka ni bilo mogoče naložiti" screen under a lock card "do 08:41" (UTC). Mobile only.
- **ROOT CAUSE (server evidence + Jest reproduction):** Caddy, this child, 08:36–08:42 UTC: `POST /api/devices` **58–60 ×200 plus 869 / 280 / 193 / 1 650 / 1 304 / 1 395 ×429 per minute**; `GET /api/child/pet` only 1–2/min, 429 because `throttle:api` (60/min per user, all `/api/*` routes) was drained by `/devices`. The loop: `mobile/src/modules/push/usePushNotifications.ts:35-37` (main @ b1e1fbf) — `Notifications.addPushTokenListener(() => { void registerForPush(); })`. On iOS `registerForPush()` → `getExpoPushTokenAsync()` → `getDevicePushTokenAsync()` → `UIApplication.registerForRemoteNotifications()` → `didRegister` resolves the promise **and emits `onDevicePushToken` again** (expo-notifications 57.0.21 `ios/ExpoNotifications/PushToken/PushTokenModule.swift#didRegister`), even for an unchanged token → the listener re-registers → forever, at network speed; `registerForPush` had no in-flight guard, no dedupe and no backoff, and a 429 didn't stop it (the echo fires before the POST). Started on the first sign-in / restore with notification permission granted (M3-02, PR #35). Jest reproduction (`modules/push/__tests__/pushRegistrationLoop.test.tsx`, mock emits the event on every token fetch like iOS): main code → **60 002 `registerDevice` calls in one simulated minute**; fixed → 1. Most likely also the iOS crash: a JS thread saturated by thousands of native round-trips / promises per minute (memory / watchdog) — confirm with the 1.24.4 crash log.
- **Push fix (`modules/push/pushRegistration.ts`):** `registerForPush()` shares one in-flight run; sends each (session token, Expo token) pair once per app run; the token listener goes through `handlePushTokenEvent(deviceToken)`, which ignores events during our own token fetch and 10 s after (the echo) and events with the already-known device token — only a real rotation re-registers; `POST /api/devices` has its own limiter (`devicesGate`: 2, then 1 per minute); failures retry in the background with `Retry-After` (429) or 30 s / 60 s backoff, max 3 attempts, never for other 4xx; state reset on logout (`unregisterFromPush`).
- **Earlier findings (before the server evidence):** (1) **No unbounded GET loop reproduces in main for this payload** — Jest with fake timers on the real in-illness / post-recovery payloads, the server answering "ill" for 20 s past the end: 0 GETs/min with the socket live, 6/min polling without it; also with the socket / AppState flapping every 1–3 s: ≤ 2/min. `nextRefreshDelay` clamps every past boundary to the 30 s retry, the illness end was not a boundary at all. (2) `throttle:api` is keyed by user id and **shared by every `/api/*` route of that child** (`GET /api/user`, `POST /api/broadcasting/auth` — re-sent by pusher-js on every reconnect —, `/api/devices`, steps, actions) and by every device logged in as that child; the four 429s are 59 s / 9 s / 37 s apart — the cadence of single requests (relaunch, retry taps), not of a loop. **To confirm on the server:** count all requests of that child token per path between 08:39 and 08:41 UTC (Caddy access log), especially `POST /api/broadcasting/auth`, and whether a second device used the same child. (3) Confirmed client defects: a 4xx (incl. 429) was never retried (`queryClient.shouldRetry`), so with no cached view the HUD stayed on the error screen until a tap; the restored lock overlay had `timezone: null` and printed the UTC wall clock of `/api/user`'s `illness_until` (`appStore.signIn`, `lockClock`); the restored lock never expires without a child state. (4) Crash: not reproducible here. The root `ErrorBoundary` in `AppNavigator` already catches render errors (it would have shown "Nekaj je šlo narobe", not an iOS crash), so the crash was outside React render — a native crash (expo-video / AVPlayer) or a JS exception thrown from a native callback / timer. Needs the TestFlight crash log (Xcode Organizer → Crashes).
- **Fix (defence in depth, `modules/childPet/refetchGovernor.ts`):** fetch gate for every child-state GET (burst 3, then 1 per 6 s ≈ ≤ 10/min; closed for `Retry-After` after a 429); gate for `POST /api/broadcasting/auth` (burst 6, 1 per 10 s) in `createEcho`; boundary refetch ≥ 30 s apart with backoff 60 s → 5 min while the state doesn't change (`viewSignature`); the vet-visit end (`lock.until`) is a boundary now; child query retries 429 / 5xx / network twice with `Retry-After` / 2 s, 4 s; error state → the hook retries itself (15 s … 2 min), the HUD keeps the last view (stale banner) and the error screen says "Poskušam znova samodejno …"; global `retryDelay` = `retryDelayFor`.
- **HUD:** `components/HudErrorBoundary.tsx` around `ChildHudScreen` in `AppNavigator` (retry remounts, cache kept).
- **Lock time zone:** `modules/session/familyTimezone.ts` remembers the family zone (SecureStore, from every child state; restored in `restoreSession` → `signIn({ familyTimezone })`; deleted on logout); `LockedScreen` → `lockClockWithFallback` (family zone → device zone → device local clock).
- **Tests:** 7 new files + additions, **950** green (84 suites, 35 new) — push loop reproduction (old code 60 002 calls/min → 1), 429 attempts ≤ 3, shared in-flight registration, real rotation once; governor unit tests, incident reproduction with the redacted production payload (`test-utils/incidentState20261006.json`, URLs stripped) counting GETs per minute (≤ 10, unlocked after the vet), forced "boundary due now" (≤ 3/min, backing off), invalidation storm every 200 ms (≤ 13 / 10 / 10 per minute), 429 with / without cached view, HUD resilience, error boundary, LockedScreen restore zone, familyTimezone store, restoreSession. `npx tsc --noEmit` clean. Jest: new `jest.afterEnv.ts` resets the gates before every test.
- **Needs a device:** dev / TestFlight build — leave the HUD at the vet across the end; airplane mode → stale banner → back online recovers without a tap; check the Xcode crash log of 1.24.4.
- **New debt:** (0) backend: rate-limit `POST /api/devices` separately (e.g. 5/min per user) so a misbehaving client can't starve the state GET; TestFlight 1.24.4 builds keep looping until updated — consider rejecting `app_version` 1.24.4 on `/api/devices` with 200 no-op server-side; (1) backend: `throttle:api` is one bucket per user for all routes — consider a separate limiter for `broadcasting/auth` and `GET /api/child/pet` (backend task); (2) the restored lock (no child state) still never expires on its own — the HUD's auto-retry fixes it once the server answers; (3) polling continues every 10 s while the query is in an error state (bounded by the gate; harmless); (4) one intermittent failure in `ChildHudScreen.test.tsx` seen in 1 of 9 full runs (real-timer `waitFor` under load, also seen on the album-lock test before this change) — watch CI; (5) exceptions from native callbacks are still fatal — consider `ErrorUtils.setGlobalHandler` reporting once a crash reporter is approved (no analytics SDK without David).

- **David — exact steps before pushes reach phones:**
  1. **iOS (APNs key):** `cd mobile && eas credentials` → iOS → profile `production` → *Push Notifications: Manage your Apple Push Notifications Key* → *Set up a Push Notification Key* (EAS creates it with your Apple Developer login, or upload an existing `.p8` + Key ID; Team ID is filled in). One key serves all builds (sandbox + production). Alternatively answer **Yes** when `eas build` asks to set up push notifications.
  2. **Android (FCM v1):** Firebase console → create / pick a project → *Add app* → Android, package `com.datavallis.petprep` → download `google-services.json`. Then **either** `cd mobile && eas env:create --name GOOGLE_SERVICES_JSON --type file --value ./google-services.json --visibility secret --environment production` (repeat for `preview` / `development`) **or** keep the file locally in `mobile/` (gitignored; picked up by `app.config.ts`). Firebase → Project settings → *Service accounts* → *Generate new private key* (JSON) → `eas credentials` → Android → *Google Service Account* → *Manage your Google Service Account Key for Push Notifications (FCM V1)* → upload that JSON.
  3. **New native build** (expo-notifications is a native module; OTA can't add it): `eas build --profile production --platform all` (TestFlight / internal) — or `--profile development` for a dev client (APNs sandbox).
  4. **Optional:** if you turn on *Enhanced security for push notifications* on expo.dev, create an access token and put `EXPO_ACCESS_TOKEN=…` in `/opt/petprep/.env`. Nothing else on the server: `PUSH_ENABLED` defaults to `true` in `compose.production.yaml`.
- **Device checks (new build):** (1) child: sign the contract → Slovenian pre-prompt → system prompt → allow → row in `device_push_tokens` (platform, version). (2) parent: add a child → parent pre-prompt. (3) Trigger: in `php artisan tinker` set a test pet's `hunger_level` to 30 (outside quiet hours) → next minute's tick → child phone gets the phase-1 text; 8 → phase 2 with sound (Android: channel "Nujna opozorila", vibration); `hunger_zero_since` 2 h ago + `hunger_level` 0 → parent alarm on every parent phone. (4) Tap: child lands on the HUD (album closed), parent on the child's detail — also from a killed app. (5) During quiet hours nothing arrives (`push_notifications.status = suppressed`, reason `quiet_hours`). (6) Logout → row gone; reinstall the app → after the next send / receipt the old token gets `disabled_at` (DeviceNotRegistered). (7) Foreground: banner shows; phase 1 silent, phase 2+ with sound.
- **New debt:** (1) a push suppressed during quiet hours is never delivered later (by design, open question to David); (2) no per-user push opt-out / settings screen (only the OS switch); (3) the parent pre-prompt shows over the PIN step right after the child is created; (4) Android notification icon = the 432 px adaptive monochrome icon (no dedicated 96 px asset); (5) no custom "whimper" sound for phase 2 (spec mentions it) — needs a bundled `.wav` + plugin `sounds` + a new build; (6) phase-2 hunger / thirst text promises illness "v 30 minutah", which the game rules don't do (open question); (7) receipts are only read for tickets < 24 h old — a scheduler outage > 24 h leaves those unchecked (closed without receipt); (8) `pint --test` fails on 9 legacy files (pre-existing).

### 2026-10-05 (cloud, mobile-engineer) — TestFlight fixes: unstyled overlays, HUD bars, realtime badge (`fix/mobile-unstyled-overlays`)
From David's TestFlight screenshot (iPhone, build from `main`).
- **1. Unstyled walk overlay → NativeWind removed.** `className` styles were dropped in the production build: babel-preset-expo 57 adds `react-native-worklets/plugin` automatically whenever the package resolves (`node_modules/babel-preset-expo/build/configs/expo.js`), and it conflicts with NativeWind's `jsxImportSource`. Only 4 files used `className` (`WalkTrackerOverlay`, `CleaningOverlay`, `LockedScreen`, `BreedPaywallScreen`) → converted to `StyleSheet` in the HUD language (dark glass cards `rgba(15,23,42,.85–.92)`, radius 24, white text, emerald / amber accents; testIDs kept, new ones added). Removed `nativewind` + `tailwindcss` (package.json + yarn.lock regenerated with `yarn remove` in an isolated copy — only their own transitive deps dropped, no version changes), `tailwind.config.js`, `nativewind-env.d.ts`, `src/global.css`, the jest mocks, `withNativeWind` (metro) and the NativeWind preset (babel). `babel.config.js` is now plain `babel-preset-expo` — the worklets plugin is added by the preset (must not be listed twice). Reanimated 4 + worklets stay installed (no app code uses them; `react-native-css-interop` was their only consumer). Guards: `src/__tests__/noClassName.test.ts` (no `className` prop, no NativeWind / Tailwind / `.css` import, no NativeWind in babel / metro / package.json) and TypeScript itself (without `nativewind-env.d.ts`, `className` is a type error).
- **2. HUD metric bars behind the dock.** The column had `top: 130` and 100 pt tracks (~750 pt) — it never fit. New pure `src/modules/hud/hudLayout.ts#computeHudLayout(window height, safe-area insets, measured header / dock heights)` → header `top`, column `top`, dock `bottom`, banner `top` and a `MetricSizing` (regular / compact / tiny, track ≤ 100 pt). Header / dock heights are measured with `onLayout` (estimates for the first frame). Insets come from `SafeAreaInsetsContext` (zero without a provider — tests). Result (estimates): iPhone SE 667 → compact, 31 pt tracks; 13 mini → compact 55; 15/16 → regular 43; Pro Max 932 → regular 63; 640 pt Android → compact 23. `MetricBar` takes `sizing` + `testID`, label one line (`numberOfLines={1}`, 9 pt mono, no letter-spacing, `adjustsFontSizeToFit`, width 58 → "ENERGIJA" no longer wraps), `maxFontSizeMultiplier={1}` on the HUD micro-texts (the height math relies on the line heights; VoiceOver reads `accessibilityLabel` "Energija 30%").
- **3. Red "BREZ POVEZAVE" while the API works — two real client bugs (not env):** (a) **pusher-js React Native build** (`dist/react-native/pusher.js`, what Metro bundles) exports `module.exports.Pusher = Pusher` — no default, no `__esModule`; Babel's interop made our default import `{ Pusher }`, the old shim took `.default` → `undefined` → `new` threw → the hook's `catch` set `disconnected` forever (parent dashboard too, `createEcho` is shared). Fixed with `resolvePusherConstructor()` (function / `{Pusher}` / `{default}`). (b) **`enabledTransports: ['wss']` with `forceTLS: true` disables every transport** in pusher-js 8: with TLS the strategy only uses the transport *named* `ws` (connecting to `wss://`), `wss` exists only in the non-TLS fallback → `isSupported()` false → state `failed` immediately. Now always `['ws', 'wss']` (Laravel's documented config). Both proven against the real RN build in Jest (`pusherReactNative.test.ts`: the old config → `failed`, the new one opens `wss://api.petprep.si:443/app/{key}?protocol=7…`). Server side checked by reading only: Caddy proxies websocket upgrades and `/app/*` to `reverb:8080`; `config/reverb.php` default port 443 / https; auth `POST /api/broadcasting/auth` (bootstrap/app.php). The agent proxy blocks `api.petprep.si`, so no live handshake test from here.

A wrong key now shows the grey icon + polling, not red.
- **Tests:** Jest **698 passed / 60 suites** (639 → +59): `hudLayout` (6 devices fit, insets, measured heights, variants, caps), `wsBadge`, `pusherReactNative` (real RN build: export shape, wss URL, old config fails, local ws, resolver shapes), `noClassName` guard, `WalkTrackerOverlay` (styles, texts, permissions, sync, close), `CleaningOverlay`, `BreedPaywallScreen`, `LockedScreen` styles, `MetricBar` sizing, HUD (badge states, four bars with computed track height, safe-area header / dock, refit after dock `onLayout`); `echoConfig` expectations corrected (`['ws','wss']`).

(2) HUD on iPhone SE (or the smallest available) and a Pro Max: all four bars + percentages + labels between the header card and the dock, "ENERGIJA" / "ČISTOČA" on one line; also with iOS "Larger Text" on. ### 2026-10-05 (cloud, backend-engineer) — PR #29 review fixes (CHANGES REQUESTED → fixed, same branch)

Child deletion turns the row on a kept (shared) pet into a **tombstone** (user_id null, started_at = contract / birth, ended_at = now). `CareScoreService::board()` keys tombstones `ended:{id}` with an `ends` map; `sharers()` counts caretakers active when the routine opened (start ≤ opens < end) → a sibling's deletion leaves the remaining child's past score unchanged; routines opening after the deletion are theirs alone. Active-only: `PetCaretaker::active()`, `Pet::caretakers()` (`wherePivotNull('ended_at')`), `caretakerRows()` (→ `caretakers_count`), `FamilyService::caretakerRecipients` / `activePetOf`, dashboard `pets[].caretakers`. Export lists tombstones as `child_id: null` + `ended_at`.
- **m2:** every deletion `DB::transaction(…, AccountDeletionService::TRANSACTION_ATTEMPTS = 3)`; `lockFamily()` locks all parents (id order) → all children (id order) → family, then pets; membership re-read after the locks (`conflict` 409 if the parent left meanwhile). **m3:** full-family purge deletes orphaned legacy children (role child, `parent_id` → a family parent, no family); `removeParent` re-points such orphans to the remaining parent. **m4:** `removeParent` deletes the leaving parent's legacy pets in other families (ledger detach + files after commit).

- **Mobile m5:** `shareFamilyExport` refuses texts > `EXPORT_SHARE_MAX_BYTES` (400 KB UTF-8, `utf8Bytes`) → "Izvoz je prevelik za deljenje kot besedilo …" (throttle already used server side — client message only). **m6:** no HTTP answer after sending → `unknown`: "Ni znano, ali je bil izbris izveden — preverite s ponovno prijavo." (child: "… preverite seznam otrok.", dashboard refetched `onSettled`).
- **Tests:** Pest `AccountDeletionTest` 38 → **47** (alternating siblings 100 → 100 + after-deletion full share + dashboard / caretakers_count, contract start kept on the tombstone, migration rollback/re-run, index + `activePetOf` ignore ended rows, transaction attempts = 3 via a Mockery proxy of the DatabaseManager (real retry can't run inside the RefreshDatabase transaction — Laravel never retries nested transactions), lock order via `DB::listen`, orphans, foreign legacy pets, failure-only per-scope throttles). ### 2026-10-05 (cloud, backend-engineer) — M4 part B: state videos at birth + own media storage (branch `feat/M4-state-videos`)
- **Models (David 2026-10-05):** defaults `AI_REFERENCE_IMAGE_PROFILE=nano_banana_pro`, `AI_STATE_VIDEO_PROFILE=kling_v3_pro`; the broken `kling_v16_legacy` profile is **deleted**; an unknown configured key falls back to the default with an error log (`MediaProfiles::configured`); deploy preflight warns about non-default pins. Schemas re-checked on fal.ai 2026-10-05: Nano Banana Pro body = `prompt, seed, num_images 1, aspect_ratio 9:16, resolution 1K, output_format jpeg, safety_tolerance 2` (no negative prompt in its schema); **Kling 3.0 Pro has no resolution / aspect parameter** — "720p" can't be requested, the clip follows the 9:16 start image at native Pro resolution (open question for David). Videos: 5 s, `generate_audio: false`.
- **M4-03 pipeline (`Media\PetMediaService`):** new table `pet_media` (one slot per pet + kind + state, `NULLS NOT DISTINCT` unique; generation counter, `source_generation`, attempts, request_id unique, source_url, storage_path, bytes, mime, duration, cost from the ledger, error_reason CHECK = `AiCallFailure`); `ai_spend_ledger.pet_media_id`. Migration copies `pet_media_jobs` rows (pending → running with the same request id; completed-never-downloaded → failed) and drops the table (reversible; tested). Flow: `GeneratePetReferenceImage` (claims the image slot; a pre-M4-05 pet with a fal URL is only downloaded, $0) → `StorePetMedia` → `media_status=ready`, `reference_image_ready` → `queueStateVideos()` per `MediaEntitlementService` (premium_unlock → 6 states, else idle + sleeping; config `media.video_states`) → `SubmitPetStateVideo` (start image = our signed URL, DNA-trait prompt + subtle motion / static camera / no people or text, video negative prompt) → signed webhook → `StorePetMedia` → `video_ready`. Atomic slot claims (+ reclaim after 10 min) make duplicates harmless; webhook of a regenerated slot → 200 `Superseded.`, but 404 while the slot still waits for its own request id (fal faster than our submit). Budget / balance / disabled → `failed` + reason (no queue retry); submit timeout after sending → `failed http_error`, cost kept; fal 5xx → queue retry. `media:retry` (renamed from `media:retry-references`, alias kept) now re-queues blocked videos after images; new hourly `media:sweep` (:47): no webhook in 2 h → `timed_out`, lost downloads / dead claims re-queued.
- **M4-05 storage:** `MediaDownloader` — allowlisted https fal hosts (also after ≤ 3 redirects), size limit (Content-Length / streaming / final; 25 MB image, 60 MB video), `finfo` sniffed type (jpeg/png/webp, mp4) + declared type must not contradict, timeout ≤ 80 s (job timeout 85 < retry_after 90), never inside a transaction. Disk `pet_media` = `storage/app/pet-media/{pet}/{reference|state}-g{n}.{ext}` (volume `app_storage`, survives deploys — confirmed in compose + both rsync excludes); old file deleted after the new one is stored; files deleted with the pet. **`GET /api/media/{media}`**: `signed:relative`, bucketed expiry (same URL 30 min, valid 60–90 min), optional Sanctum token must pass `PetPolicy::listen`, BinaryFileResponse with Range (AVPlayer), `nosniff`, throttle `media` 240/min/IP. Apps never get fal URLs.
- **API:** `media {status: disabled|pending|failed|partial|ready, reference_image_url, videos {state: url} (JSON object), current_video_url (pet_state → fallback idle), states, expires_at}` (`PetMediaPayload`, typed for Scramble) in `ChildPetStateResource.pet`, `PairedPetResource`, dashboard legacy `pet` (+ `reference_image_url`, `media_status`), every `family.pets[]`, and `PetUpdated`; legacy `current_video_url` / `reference_image_url` mirror it. `mobile/src/api/schema.ts` regenerated; mobile `PetMedia` type + `PetUpdatedBroadcast.media?`, fixtures `makeMedia()`.
- **Filament:** PetResource edit → **"AI media"** panel (superadmin): thumbnail, open link, status + reason, profile, generation, est. cost, size; **Regenerate** (old file stays until the new one is stored; image regen → videos follow) and **Generate missing**; the editable `current_video_url` field removed. **`php artisan media:backfill {--pet=} {--limit=50} {--dry-run}`** — plan per pet (image ok / download / generate, missing videos, est. cost), stops at the budget.

Reference image job: budget / balance / disabled → `media_status=failed` + new `pets.media_error`, **no retry**, pet fully playable; other errors retry as before. Filament widget `AiSpendOverview` (dashboard + lab), `PetResource` shows media status + error.
- **Pet DNA v2 (M4-08):** `Media\PetDnaService` + `config/breed_appearance.php` (mutt broad mixed set, border collie) — **all `verified: false`, a Claude draft; replace in M1-19 from FCI No. 297 / AKC**. Seed = crc32(pet id + random salt), assigned right after the insert in the pairing transaction (family row locked) → per family + breed no duplicate `trait_fingerprint` (25 attempts). Prompt (`PetAppearancePrompt`) = breed + traits + photo style + "no people, no children, no text, no watermark"; negative prompt stored and sent where the model supports it. `prompt_anchor` / `visual_traits` keep the v1 keys, so the job and API resources are unchanged. Existing pets keep v1; `AI_PET_DNA_VERSION=1` switches new pets back.
- **AI Lab:** Filament page **`/admin/ai-lab`** (superadmin only, `AI_LAB_ENABLED`), tables `media_lab_runs` / `media_lab_results`, jobs `RunMediaLabImage` (sync, no retry, claim queued→running), `SubmitMediaLabVideo` (fal queue + the existing signed webhook — `FalAiWebhookController` now also finishes lab results by `request_id`), `PollMediaLabResult` ("Check pending" when the webhook can't reach the server, e.g. local dev).

(1) *Images*: pick breed, samples (1–4), tick image models, optionally fix traits (e.g. coat colour) → the estimate shows → "Generate images". Each sample = one random dog drawn by every ticked model → compare side by side (latency + cost under each image; prompt and traits in "prompt & traits"). (2) *Video*: choose a finished lab image, a state (idle / sleeping / low_energy / hungry / sick / playing) and video models → "Animate"; videos appear when fal calls the webhook (1–3 min; production only — locally use "Check pending"). The page refreshes itself every 10 s while something is pending. **Cost examples:** 4 samples × all 4 image models ≈ 4 × (0.006 + 0.03 + 0.15 + 0.035) = **$0.88**; one image × all 4 video models ≈ 0.56 + 0.35 + 0.40 + 0.12 = **$1.43**. Per-run limit $3 (`AI_LAB_MAX_RUN_USD`); the lab has its own budget $3/day and $30/month (`AI_LAB_DAILY_USD` / `AI_LAB_MONTHLY_USD`, since the PR #22 review), separate from the pets' $5/day and $50/month. Production needs `FAL_AI_API_KEY` on the server (not set yet) and the `queue` worker.

Webhook helpers moved from `FalAiTest.php` to `tests/Pest.php`. Backend **684 passed** (637 → +47) on own DB `testing_m4a`; Pint clean on touched files. No API route / payload change for the apps → no `schema.ts` regen.

### 2026-10-04 (cloud, backend-engineer) — M2-06 routines / Care Score / traffic light + M2-05 dashboard backend (`feat/M2-05-dashboard-scoring`)
- **David's rules (DECISIONS, PRODUCT_SPEC §9 / §11):** routines = each feed window, `water_times_per_day` refills per family day, each hygiene event (clean within 2 h outside quiet hours), the daily step goal; expected only while born, active, not hard-stopped, not at the vet, not game over; birth day only after birth. Care Score = done / expected × 100 − 10 per illness (0–100). Shared pet = "fair share" (1/n per routine for the n caretakers caring when it opened; own done; −10 per illness during their caretaking). Traffic light per child and pet, today: red = game over / phase 3 / ill today; yellow = > 2 missed today; green.
- **Claude's interpretations (pending David, DECISIONS):** walk credit for a sibling = goal met AND own steps ≥ goal/n; a missed routine of a shared pet makes **every** caretaker yellow; a feed window entirely in quiet hours is not expected; a routine overlapping a hard stop / vet / game over is not expected unless done, water pro-rated by in-play non-quiet time; child shares routines that open after their contract; past day without step data → no walk routine; "ill today" = illness started today; ledger starts 2026-10-04; pending routines not scored.
- **Code:** `RoutineLedgerService` (derives routines; `closeDueDays()` at the end of every `pets:process-decay` materialises final days into new `pet_daily_routines`, idempotent, pointer `pets.routines_closed_through` / `routines_next_close_at`), `CareScoreService` (board, pet / child scores, lights, day blocks, progress), `PetStatusPeriodService` + new `pet_status_periods` (history of hard stop / illness / inactive written from `Pet` created/updated hooks; backfilled from −1 / −2 activity rows and current flags), `CareScheduleService::feedWindowsStartingOn`, `FamilyDashboardService` (children / pets blocks, timelines in one window-function query, `childReport`), `ChildReportController` + `ChildReportRequest` → `GET /api/parent/children/{child}/report?days=7|30|84`. Migration `2026_10_05_120000_create_routine_ledger`.

### 2026-10-04 (cloud, mobile-engineer) — PR #18 re-review (APPROVED) follow-ups N1–N5 (same branch)
- **N1:** the backend sends `illness_until` / `born_at` / `updated_at` as `toIso8601String()` in UTC (`…+00:00`) and `emitted_at` as `toRfc3339String(true)` (`…250+00:00`). `lockClock` uses a string's own clock time only when its offset equals the family zone's offset at that instant (or the zone can't be resolved); otherwise Intl in `state.timezone` (`16:30:00+00:00` → "18:30" in Ljubljana, "12:30" in New York, "17:30" in London in October). Test fixtures (`makeBroadcast`, broadcast literals) now use the real `+00:00` format.
- **N2:** every HTTP state stores `clockSkewMs` (server − device clock on arrival); `nextRefreshDelay(view, deviceNow)` compares feed-window / water / family-midnight boundaries in server time, so a fast or slow phone clock no longer refreshes too early / late; while a boundary has passed but the state still blocks the action (and not because of a lock or a mess), it retries every 30 s.
- **N3:** the Android counter is persisted only while its child is still signed in — `logout()` deletes the key, and the HUD unmount no longer writes it back (test + a positive control for a normal unmount).
- **N4:** one cached `Intl.DateTimeFormat` per zone (`familyTime.ts`), shared by clock formatting and offset lookup.
- **N5:** stale "suggested mobile/CLAUDE.md edit" line removed (the coordinator updated the manual).
- **Tests:** Jest **425 passed / 37 suites** (418 → +7), twice; `tsc --noEmit` clean.

### 2026-10-04 (cloud, mobile-engineer) — PR #18 review fixes (CHANGES REQUESTED → fixed, same branch)
- **B1 (no yesterday steps credited):** Android `LiveStepCounter.raiseTo(steps, serverDay)` only applies the server's `my_steps_today` when it belongs to the same **family-local** day as the counter (date of `state.server_time` in `state.timezone`); iOS / Android compare "already on the server" only for the same family day (a cached count from before midnight never blocks today's steps). A broadcast that lowers `energy_level` (midnight reset) refetches `['child','pet']`; the M3 timer also fires at the next family midnight.
- **M1 (step day = family-local day):** new `familyCalendar(timezone, server_time)` in `modules/childPet/familyTime.ts` (Intl offsets, DST-aware, falls back to the server instant's offset). iOS reads `getStepCountAsync(family midnight, now)`; the Android counter keys / rolls over by family date. Tests run with the device clock on **UTC** (`jest.config.js` sets `TZ=UTC` for every worker — results no longer depend on the developer's zone) against Europe/Ljubljana and America/New_York families.
- **M2:** Android total stored per child (`petprep_live_steps_today_{userId}`) and deleted in `logout()`. **Limitation (to discuss with David):** iOS CoreMotion counts the whole device — on a phone shared by siblings every child who signs in reports the device's steps since the family midnight (the server's max-per-child keeps it from double counting one child, not from crediting the same walk to two children). Android shares the same sensor, but only counts while that child is signed in with the app open.
- **M3:** `useChildPet` schedules one `invalidateQueries` at the earliest of `next_feed_window.start`, `current_window.end`, `water.next_allowed_at`, the next family midnight (+1 s margin; cleared on unmount / new state) — buttons re-enable without an event or a poll.
- **M4:** an action that fails without a server state (offline / 5xx / 429) restores only its metric (unless a newer broadcast already brought the server's value) and its `can_*` / `fed_*` flags on top of the current cache, then refetches — a broadcast that arrived during the request is no longer lost.
- **m1** `mergePolledState` drops an HTTP snapshot older than the shown one. **m2** the Android counter loads only after the previous counter's `persist()` finished, then syncs once. **m3** LockedScreen uses `lock.until`'s own wall clock (family offset) and keeps the server's `until` while the illness lasts; Intl only for UTC broadcast values. **m4** closing the walk overlay syncs at once. **m5** mutation answers are written only if the same child is still signed in (user id captured at start).
- **Tests:** Jest **418 passed / 37 suites** (389 → +29), twice in a row; `tsc --noEmit` clean. New: family calendar (Ljubljana / New York / DST 25 Oct / fallback), B1 iOS + Android midnight-in-background, family-midnight iOS start for both zones, m2 persist→load order, per-user key + logout delete, M3 timer (water gap, midnight, unmount, reschedule), M4 with a broadcast during a failed feed, m1, m3, m4 (walk close), m5 (feed and steps after a session change), energy-drop refetch.
